DB-backed authentication\n23\t *    Mosquitto calls HTTP endpoints (/mqtt/auth, /mqtt/acl, /mqtt/superuser)\n24\t *    Credentials verified against edge_devices.mqtt_password_hash in database\n25\t *    Cross-tenant ACL enforced by matching device's tenant_id against topic\n26\t *    No file I/O, no locks, credentials participate in DB transactions\n27\t *\n28\t * 2. \"file\" (legacy) - our service verifies it, so use OWASP-recommended count\n56\t  // File mode: Mosquitto's password_file parser needs to handle the hash, keep at 101 for compatibility\n57\t  private static readonly HTTP_MODE_ITERATIONS = 600_000;\n58\t  private static readonly FILE_MODE_ITERATIONS = 101;\n59\t\n60\t  // In-memory cache: mqttClientId → tenantId (prevents N+1 DB queries on ACL checks)\n61\t  // Max 10,000 entries with LRU-style eviction to prevent unbounded memory growth (LOW-02)\n62\t  private readonly tenantIdCache = new Map<string, { tenantId: string; expiresAt: number }>();\n63\t  private readonly TENANT_CACHE_TTL_MS = 300_000; // 5 minutes\n64\t  private static readonly TENANT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE = 10_000;\n65\t\n66\t  // SENSOR-MEDIUM-004: negative-result cache for cross-schema device lookups.\n67\t  // A lookup that misses BOTH the O(1) directory and the fallback UNION-ALL scan\n68\t  // is recorded here (keyed `${column}:${value}`) for a short window, so a flood\n69\t  // of the same unknown identifier is not re-scanned across every tenant schema.\n70\t  // It lives in findDeviceAcrossSchemas so EVERY public entry point benefits —\n71\t  // the unauthenticated verifyDeviceCredentials (MQTT CONNECT) and the ACL\n72\t  // own-device check, not just getDeviceTenantId. Short TTL so a freshly\n73\t  // provisioned device becomes resolvable quickly; bounded with LRU eviction so\n74\t  // the flood cannot itself grow memory unboundedly.\n75\t  private readonly negativeLookupCache = new Map<string, number>(); // `${column}:${value}` → expiresAt\n76\t  private readonly NEGATIVE_LOOKUP_CACHE_TTL_MS = 30_000; // 30 seconds\n77\t  private static readonly NEGATIVE_LOOKUP_CACHE_MAX_SIZE = 10_000;\n78\t\n79\t  constructor(\n80\t    private readonly configService: ConfigService,\n81\t    @InjectRepository(EdgeDevice)\n82\t    private readonly deviceRepository: Repository<EdgeDevice>,\n83\t    private readonly dataSource: DataSource,\n84\t    private readonly deviceDirectory: DeviceDirectoryService,\n85\t  ) {\n86\t    // SENSOR-LOW-008: default to the DB-backed HTTP backend. The\n112\t    // 101-iteration file-mode hash is unacceptable for a production trust\n113\t    // boundary; starting in it must be an explicit, audited operator decision\n114\t    // (MQTT_ALLOW_LEGACY_FILE_MODE=true) during a migration window, never a\n115\t    // silent default.\n116\t    const isProduction = this.configService.get<string>('NODE_ENV') === 'production';\n117\t    const legacyFileModeAllowed =\n118\t      this.configService.get<string>('MQTT_ALLOW_LEGACY_FILE_MODE') === 'true';\n119\t    if (isProduction && this.authMode === 'file' && !legacyFileModeAllowed) {\n120\t      throw new Error("}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"be4c4031-0af4-45d9-84b3-2588b84b2aef","uuid":"a873b1eb-4f49-4f12-90d9-91ef74d57eef","timestamp":"2026-09-25T08:50:45.688Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-5ccb419a44c1/apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/mqtt-auth.service.ts","content":"import { execFile } from 'child_process';\nimport { pbkdf2Sync, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual, createHash } from 'crypto';\nimport { promises as fs } from 'fs';\nimport { promisify } from 'util';\n\nimport { Injectable, Logger, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';\nimport { DataSource, Repository } from 'typeorm';\nimport { getTenantSchemaName } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/database';\n\nimport { DeviceDirectoryService } from './device-directory.service';\nimport { EdgeDevice } from './entities/edge-device.entity';\n\nconst execFileAsync = promisify(execFile);\n\n/**\n * MQTT Authentication Service\n *\n * Supports two modes (configured via MQTT_AUTH_MODE env var):\n *\n * 1. \"http\" (recommended for production) - DB-backed authentication\n *    Mosquitto calls HTTP endpoints (/mqtt/auth, /mqtt/acl, /mqtt/superuser)\n *    Credentials verified against edge_devices.mqtt_password_hash in database\n *    Cross-tenant ACL enforced by matching device's tenant_id against topic\n *    No file I/O, no locks, credentials participate in DB transactions\n *\n * 2. \"file\" (legacy) - File-based password_file authentication\n *    Credentials written to Mosquitto password file on disk\n *    Requires atomic writes, file locks, and SIGHUP reload\n *    ACL limited to Mosquitto's built-in pattern matching\n *\n * Service accounts (backend_service, sensor_service, alert_service) are\n * verified via environment variable hashes in both modes.\n */\n@Injectable()\nexport class MqttAuthService implements OnModuleInit {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(MqttAuthService.name);\n\n  // Auth mode: \"http\" (DB-backed) or \"file\" (legacy file-based)\n  private readonly authMode: 'http' | 'file';\n\n  // File-based mode settings (legacy)\n  private readonly passwordFilePath: string;\n  private readonly fileAuthEnabled: boolean;\n  private writeLock: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();\n\n  // Service account credentials (hashes from env vars)\n  private readonly serviceAccounts: Map<string, string> = new Map();\n\n  // Service accounts with per-topic-pattern grants (no superuser access)\n  private readonly serviceAccountNames = new Set(['backend_service', 'sensor_service', 'alert_service']);\n\n  // PBKDF2 iteration counts per auth mode\n  // HTTP mode: Mosquitto never parses the hash - our service verifies it, so use OWASP-recommended count\n  // File mode: Mosquitto's password_file parser needs to handle the hash, keep at 101 for compatibility\n  private static readonly HTTP_MODE_ITERATIONS = 600_000;\n  private static readonly FILE_MODE_ITERATIONS = 101;\n\n  // In-memory cache: mqttClientId → tenantId (prevents N+1 DB queries on ACL checks)\n  // Max 10,000 entries with LRU-style eviction to prevent unbounded memory growth (LOW-02)\n  private readonly tenantIdCache = new Map<string, { tenantId: string; expiresAt: number }>();\n  private readonly TENANT_CACHE_TTL_MS = 300_000; // 5 minutes\n  private static readonly TENANT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE = 10_000;\n\n  // SENSOR-MEDIUM-004: negative-result cache for cross-schema device lookups.\n  // A lookup that misses BOTH the O(1) directory and the fallback UNION-ALL scan\n  // is recorded here (keyed `${column}:${value}`) for a short window, so a flood\n  // of the same unknown identifier is not re-scanned across every tenant schema.\n  // It lives in findDeviceAcrossSchemas so EVERY public entry point benefits —\n  // the unauthenticated verifyDeviceCredentials (MQTT CONNECT) and the ACL\n  // own-device check, not just getDeviceTenantId. Short TTL so a freshly\n  // provisioned device becomes resolvable quickly; bounded with LRU eviction so\n  // the flood cannot itself grow memory unboundedly.\n  private readonly negativeLookupCache = new Map<string, number>(); // `${column}:${value}` → expiresAt\n  private readonly NEGATIVE_LOOKUP_CACHE_TTL_MS = 30_000; // 30 seconds\n  private static readonly NEGATIVE_LOOKUP_CACHE_MAX_SIZE = 10_000;\n\n  constructor(\n    private readonly configService: ConfigService,\n    @InjectRepository(EdgeDevice)\n    private readonly deviceRepository: Repository<EdgeDevice>,\n    private readonly dataSource: DataSource,\n    private readonly deviceDirectory: DeviceDirectoryService,\n  ) {\n    // SENSOR-LOW-008: default to the DB-backed HTTP backend. File mode hashes\n    // at only 101 PBKDF2 iterations (Mosquitto password_file parser limit),\n    // orders of magnitude below OWASP guidance; HTTP mode uses 600k. Secure by\n    // default (Tier-2) — legacy file mode must now be opted into explicitly.\n    this.authMode = this.configService.get<string>('MQTT_AUTH_MODE', 'http') as 'http' | 'file';\n\n    // File-based settings\n    this.passwordFilePath = this.configService.get<string>(\n      'MOSQUITTO_PASSWORD_FILE',\n      'infrastructure/simulators/mosquitto/config/passwd',\n    );\n    this.fileAuthEnabled = this.configService.get<boolean>('MQTT_AUTH_ENABLED', false);\n\n    // Load service account hashes from env\n    const serviceHashes: [string, string | undefined][] = [\n      ['backend_service', this.configService.get<string>('MQTT_BACKEND_SERVICE_HASH')],\n      ['sensor_service', this.configService.get<string>('MQTT_SENSOR_SERVICE_HASH')],\n      ['alert_service', this.configService.get<string>('MQTT_ALERT_SERVICE_HASH')],\n    ];\n    for (const [name, hash] of serviceHashes) {\n      if (hash) this.serviceAccounts.set(name, hash);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async onModuleInit(): Promise<void> {\n    // SENSOR-LOW-008: fail closed on weak legacy file mode in production. The\n    // 101-iteration file-mode hash is unacceptable for a production trust\n    // boundary; starting in it must be an explicit, audited operator decision\n    // (MQTT_ALLOW_LEGACY_FILE_MODE=true) during a migration window, never a\n    // silent default.\n    const isProduction = this.configService.get<string>('NODE_ENV') === 'production';\n    const legacyFileModeAllowed =\n      this.configService.get<string>('MQTT_ALLOW_LEGACY_FILE_MODE') === 'true';\n    if (isProduction && this.authMode === 'file' && !legacyFileModeAllowed) {\n      throw new Error(","numLines":120,"startLine":1,"totalLines":825}}}

(topic.startsWith('$SYS/') && acc === 1) return true;\n        return false;\n\n      case 'alert_service':\n        // alert_service: read sensor/device data, write alerts (tenant-scoped only)\n    // Legacy topics: restricted to read-only, non-production only\n        if (this.configService.get('NODE_ENV') !== 'production') {\n          if ((topic.startsWith('sensor/') ||    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Look up a device's tenantId, using an in-memory cache to avoid repeated DB queries.\n   * ACL checks happen on every PUBLISH/SUBSCRIBE, so caching is critical.\n   *\n   * Security (LOW-02):\n   * - Rejects client IDs that do not match the expected 'edge-{...}-{...}' format early,\n   *   preventing repeated DB queries from random-client-ID floods.\n   * - Enforces a that cannot possibly be valid edge device identifiers\n    // Expected format: edge-{uuid}-{deviceCode} or similar structured prefix\n    if (!username.startsWith('edge-') && !this.serviceAccountNames.has(username)) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    const now = Date.now();\n    const cached = this.tenantIdCache.get(username);\n    if (cached && now < cached.expiresAt) {\n      // Move to end (most-recently-used) by re-inserting\n      this.tenantIdCache.delete(username);\n      this.tenantIdCache.set(username, cached);\n      return cached.tenantId;\n    }\n\n    // SENSOR-MEDIUM-004: the negative-result cache now lives one level down in\n    // findDeviceAcrossSchemas, so an unknown-username flood is bounded here AND\n    // on the unauthenticated auth/own-device paths.\n    const device = await this.findDeviceAcrossSchemas('mqtt_client_id', username);\n\n    if (!device?.tenantId) {\n      this.tenantIdCache.delete(username); // drop any stale positive entry\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    // Enforce max cache size: evict the oldest entry (first inserted) before adding new one\n    if (this.tenantIdCache.size >= MqttAuthService.TENANT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE) {\n      const oldestKey = this.tenantIdCache.keys().next().value;\n      if (oldestKey) {\n        this.tenantIdCache.delete(oldestKey);\n      }\n    }\n\n    this.tenantIdCache.set(username, {\n      tenantId: device.tenantId,\n      expiresAt: now + this.TENANT_CACHE_TTL_MS,\n    });\n\n    return device.tenantId;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Invalidate the tenant cache for a device (call when device is revoked/decommissioned).\n   */\n  /**\n   * Task 1.8 (erasure): drop EVERY cache entry mapping to one tenant.\n   * Returns the number of dropped entries so the erasure hook can log\n   * the blast radius. The lookup key is column-qualified.\n438\t    this.negativeLookupCache.delete(`mqtt_client_id:${username}`);\n439\t  }\n440\t\n441\t  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n442\t  // Cross-Schema Device Lookup\n443\t  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n444\t\n445\t  /**\n446\t   * Resolve a device by a public identifier without tenant context.\n447\t   *\n448\t   * SENSOR-MEDIUM-004: consult the O(1) sensor.edge_device_directory first and,\n449\t   * on a hit, issue a single targeted query against the owning tenant's\n450\t   * edge_devices. This removes the per-request cross-schema fan-out\n453\t   * that the un-rate-limited MQTT-auth path could be driven into as a DoS.\n454\t   */\n455\t  private async findDeviceAcrossSchemas(\n456\t    column: 'mqtt_client_id' | 'id',\n457\t    value: string,\n458\t  ): Promise<EdgeDevice | null> {\n459\t    // SENSOR-MEDIUM-004: short-circuit recently-confirmed-absent identifiers so a\n460\t    // flood of the same unknown value (auth CONNECT, ACL, own-device) does not\n461\t    // re-scan every tenant schema. Bounds the DoS on the unauthenticated path.\n462\t    const negativeKey = `${column}:${value}`;\n463\t    const now = Date.now();\n464\t    const negativeExpiry = this.negativeLookupCache.get(negativeKey);\n465\t    if (negativeExpiry !== undefined) {\n466\t      if (now < negativeExpiry) {\n467\t        return null;\n468\t      }\n469\t      this.negativeLookupCache.delete(negativeKey);\n470\t    }\n471\t\n472\t    const tenantId = await this.deviceDirectory.lookupTenantId(column, value);\n473\t    if (tenantId) {\n474\t      const schema = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n475\t      const rows = await this.dataSource.query(\n476\t        `SELECT * FROM \"${schema}\".edge_devices WHERE \"${column}\" = $1 LIMIT 1`,\n477\t        [value],\n478\t      );\n479\t      if (rows && rows.length > 0) {\n480\t        return this.mapRowToEdgeDevice(rows[0]);\n481\t      }\n482\t      // Directory pointed at a tenant that no longer holds the row (moved /\n483\t      // deleted): fall through to the authoritative scan.\n484\t    }\n485\t\n486\t    const device = await this.scanDeviceAcrossSchemas(column, value);\n487\t    if (device) {\n488\t      await this.deviceDirectory.backfill({\n489\t        deviceId: device.id,\n490\t        deviceCode: device.deviceCode,\n491\t        mqttClientId: device.mqttClientId ?? MqttAuthService.HTTP_MODE_ITERATIONS\n568\t      : MqttAuthService.FILE_MODE_ITERATIONS;\n569\t    const hash = this.hashPassword(password, iterations);\n570\t    return { password, hash };\n571\t  }\n572\t\n573\t  /**\n574\t   * Hash a password using PBKDF2-SHA512 (Mosquitto $7$ format).\n575\t   * Format: $7$iterations$base64salt$base64hash\n576\t   *\n577\t   * SENSOR-LOW-008: the default is the OWASP-grade HTTP-mode count; the weak\n578\t   * 101-iteration file-mode value must be passed explicitly by the legacy path.\n579\t   */\n580\t  hashPassword(password: string, iterations: number = MqttAuthService.HTTP_MODE_ITERATIONS): string {\n581\t    const salt = randomBytes(12);\n582\t    const keyLength = 24;\n583\t    const derivedKey = pbkdf2Sync(password, salt, iterations, keyLength, 'sha512');\n584\t    return `$7$${iterations}$${salt.toString('base64')}$${derivedKey.toString('base64')}`;\n585\t  }\n586\t\n587\t  /**\n588\t   * Verify a password against a PBKDF2-SHA512 hash.\n589\t   * Uses timing-safe comparison to prevent timing attacks.\n590\t   */\n591\t  verifyPassword(password: string, hash: string): boolean {\n592\t    try {\n593\t      const parts = hash.split('$');\n594\t      if (parts.length !== 5 || parts[1] !== '7') {\n595\t        return false;\n596\t      }\n597\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"be4c4031-0af4-45d9-84b3-2588b84b2aef","uuid":"d49e948c-73cc-4b0f-818d-b04a3abac783","timestamp":"2026-09-25T08:50:50.656Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-5ccb419a44c1/apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/mqtt-auth.service.ts","content":"   * Returns the number of dropped entries so the erasure hook can log\n   * the blast radius. The lookup key is column-qualified.\n    this.negativeLookupCache.delete(`mqtt_client_id:${username}`);\n  }\n\n  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  // Cross-Schema Device Lookup\n  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  /**\n   * Resolve a device by a public identifier without tenant context.\n   *\n   * SENSOR-MEDIUM-004: consult the O(1) sensor.edge_device_directory first and,\n   * on a hit, issue a single targeted query against the owning tenant's\n   * edge_devices. Only a directory miss (or a stale entry) falls back to the\n   * O(number-of-tenants) UNION-ALL scan, which then backfills the directory so\n   * the next lookup is O(1). This removes the per-request cross-schema fan-out\n   * that the un-rate-limited MQTT-auth path could be driven into as a DoS.\n   */\n  private async findDeviceAcrossSchemas(\n    column: 'mqtt_client_id' | 'id',\n    value: string,\n  ): Promise<EdgeDevice | null> {\n    // SENSOR-MEDIUM-004: short-circuit recently-confirmed-absent identifiers so a\n    // flood of the same unknown value (auth CONNECT, ACL, own-device) does not\n    // re-scan every tenant schema. Bounds the DoS on the unauthenticated path.\n    const negativeKey = `${column}:${value}`;\n    const now = Date.now();\n    const negativeExpiry = this.negativeLookupCache.get(negativeKey);\n    if (negativeExpiry !== undefined) {\n      if (now < negativeExpiry) {\n        return null;\n      }\n      this.negativeLookupCache.delete(negativeKey);\n    }\n\n    const tenantId = await this.deviceDirectory.lookupTenantId(column, value);\n    if (tenantId) {\n      const schema = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n      const rows = await this.dataSource.query(\n        `SELECT * FROM \"${schema}\".edge_devices WHERE \"${column}\" = $1 LIMIT 1`,\n        [value],\n      );\n      if (rows && rows.length > 0) {\n        return this.mapRowToEdgeDevice(rows[0]);\n      }\n      // Directory pointed at a tenant that no longer holds the row (moved /\n      // deleted): fall through to the authoritative scan.\n    }\n\n    const device = await this.scanDeviceAcrossSchemas(column, value);\n    if (device) {\n      await this.deviceDirectory.backfill({\n        deviceId: device.id,\n        deviceCode: device.deviceCode,\n        mqttClientId: device.mqttClientId ?? null,\n        tenantId: device.tenantId,\n      });\n      return device;\n    }\n\n    // Confirmed absent by both the directory and the scan: record a bounded,\n    // short-lived negative so repeated lookups of this identifier stay O(1).\n    if (this.negativeLookupCache.size >= MqttAuthService.NEGATIVE_LOOKUP_CACHE_MAX_SIZE) {\n      const oldest = this.negativeLookupCache.keys().next().value;\n      if (oldest !== undefined) {\n        this.negativeLookupCache.delete(oldest);\n      }\n    }\n    this.negativeLookupCache.set(negativeKey, now + this.NEGATIVE_LOOKUP_CACHE_TTL_MS);\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Authoritative fallback: UNION-ALL scan of edge_devices across every tenant\n   * schema. Used only when the directory misses.\n   */\n  private async scanDeviceAcrossSchemas(\n    column: 'mqtt_client_id' | 'id',\n    value: string,\n  ): Promise<EdgeDevice | null> {\n    const schemas: { schema_name: string }[] = await this.dataSource.query(\n      `SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata WHERE schema_name ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'`,\n    );\n\n    if (schemas.length === 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    const unionParts = schemas.map(\n      (s) => `SELECT * FROM \"${s.schema_name}\".edge_devices WHERE \"${column}\" = $1`,\n    );\n    const sql = `(${unionParts.join(' UNION ALL ')}) LIMIT 1`;\n\n    const rows = await this.dataSource.query(sql, [value]);\n\n    if (!rows || rows.length === 0) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return this.mapRowToEdgeDevice(rows[0]);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Map a raw database row (snake_case) to an EdgeDevice entity (camelCase).\n   */\n  private mapRowToEdgeDevice(row: Record<string, any>): EdgeDevice {\n    const device = new EdgeDevice();\n    device.id = row['id'];\n    device.tenantId = row['tenant_id'];\n    device.deviceCode = row['device_code'];\n    device.deviceName = row['device_name'];\n    device.lifecycleState = row['lifecycle_state'];\n    device.mqttClientId = row['mqtt_client_id'];\n    device.mqttPasswordHash = row['mqtt_password_hash'];\n    device.isOnline = row['is_online'];\n    device.lastSeenAt = row['last_seen_at'] ? MqttAuthService.HTTP_MODE_ITERATIONS\n      : MqttAuthService.FILE_MODE_ITERATIONS;\n    const hash = this.hashPassword(password, iterations);\n    return { password, hash };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Hash a password using PBKDF2-SHA512 (Mosquitto $7$ format).\n   * Format: $7$iterations$base64salt$base64hash\n   *\n   * SENSOR-LOW-008: the default is the OWASP-grade HTTP-mode count; the weak\n   * 101-iteration file-mode value must be passed explicitly by the legacy path.\n   */\n  hashPassword(password: string, iterations: number = MqttAuthService.HTTP_MODE_ITERATIONS): string {\n    const salt = randomBytes(12);\n    const keyLength = 24;\n    const derivedKey = pbkdf2Sync(password, salt, iterations, keyLength, 'sha512');\n    return `$7$${iterations}$${salt.toString('base64')}$${derivedKey.toString('base64')}`;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Verify a password against a PBKDF2-SHA512 hash.\n   * Uses timing-safe comparison to prevent timing attacks.\n   */\n  verifyPassword(password: string, hash: string): boolean {\n    try {\n      const parts = hash.split('$');\n      if (parts.length !== 5 || parts[1] !== '7') {\n        return false;\n      }\n","numLines":180,"startLine":418,"totalLines":825}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NYQPgr6cPh5xUA1e4De1bk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\n2\timport { DataSource, EntityManager } from 'typeorm';\n3\t\n4\t/** Public identifier columns the directory can resolve a tenant by. */\n5\texport type DirectoryLookupColumn = 'device_code' | 'mqtt_client_id' | 'id';\n6\t\n7\t/** A row to (re)publish into the directory. */\n8\texport interface DeviceDirectoryEntry {\n9\t  deviceId: string;\n10\t  deviceCode: string;\n11\t  mqttClientId?: string | null;\n12\t  tenantId: string;\n13\t}\n14\t\n15\t/**\n16\t * Device Directory Service (SENSOR-MEDIUM-004)\n17\t *\n18\t * Owns the cross-tenant `sensor.edge_device_directory` index that maps a\n19\t * device's public identifiers to its owning tenant in O(1). Public\n20\t * provisioning + MQTT-auth endpoints use it to resolve the tenant with a single\n21\t * indexed query instead of a UNION-ALL scan across every tenant schema.\n22\t *\n23\t * The directory is a routing hint, not the source of truth: writers keep it in\n24\t * sync inside the same transaction that mutates `edge_devices`, and readers\n25\t * treat a miss as \"fall back to the scan and backfill me\".\n26\t */\n27\t@Injectable()\n28\texport class DeviceDirectoryService {\n29\t  private readonly logger = new Logger(DeviceDirectoryService.name);\n30\t\n31\t  // The directory column that a given edge_devices lookup column maps to.\n32\t  private static readonly COLUMN_MAP: Record<DirectoryLookupColumn, string> = {\n33\t    device_code: 'device_code',\n34\t    mqtt_client_id: 'mqtt_client_id',\n35\t    id: 'device_id',\n36\t  };\n37\t\n38\t  constructor(private readonly dataSource: DataSource) {}\n39\t\n40\t  /**\n41\t   * Resolve the owning tenantId for a device by one of its public identifiers,\n42\t   * in O(1) via the directory index. Returns null on a miss (caller falls back\n43\t   * to the cross-schema scan).\n44\t   */\n45\t  async lookupTenantId(column: DirectoryLookupColumn, value: string): Promise<string | null> {\n46\t    const dirColumn = DeviceDirectoryService.COLUMN_MAP[column];\n47\t    const rows: { tenant_id: string }[] = await this.dataSource.query(\n48\t      `SELECT tenant_id FROM sensor.edge_device_directory WHERE \"${dirColumn}\" = $1 LIMIT 1`,\n49\t      [value],\n50\t    );\n51\t    return rows[0]?.tenant_id ?? null;\n52\t  }\n53\t\n54\t  /**\n55\t   * Insert or refresh the directory row for a device. Keyed on device_id so a\n56\t   * re-registration or identifier change updates in place. Runs inside the\n57\t   * caller's transaction when a manager is supplied.\n58\t   */\n59\t  async upsert(entry: DeviceDirectoryEntry, manager?: EntityManager): Promise<void> {\n60\t    const runner = manager ?? this.dataSource.manager;\n61\t    await runner.query(\n62\t      `INSERT INTO sensor.edge_device_directory\n63\t         (device_id, device_code, mqtt_client_id, tenant_id, updated_at)\n64\t       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, now())\n65\t       ON CONFLICT (device_id) DO UPDATE SET\n66\t         device_code = EXCLUDED.device_code,\n67\t         mqtt_client_id = EXCLUDED.mqtt_client_id,\n68\t         tenant_id = EXCLUDED.tenant_id,\n69\t         updated_at = now()`,\n70\t      [entry.deviceId, entry.deviceCode, entry.mqttClientId ?? null, entry.tenantId],\n71\t    );\n72\t  }\n73\t\n74\t  /**\n75\t   * Best-effort backfill from a device row a scan just resolved. Never throws —\n76\t   * the caller already has its answer; a directory hiccup must not fail the\n77\t   * request. Self-heals the directory for the next lookup.\n78\t   */\n79\t  async backfill(entry: DeviceDirectoryEntry): Promise<void> {\n80\t    try {\n81\t      await this.upsert(entry);\n82\t    } catch (error) {\n83\t      this.logger.warn(\n84\t        `Directory backfill failed for device ${entry.deviceId}: ${(error as Error).message}`,\n85\t      );\n86\t    }\n87\t  }\n88\t\n89\t  /** Remove a device from the directory (decommission / hard delete). */\n90\t  async remove(deviceId: string, manager?: EntityManager): Promise<void> {"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"be4c4031-0af4-45d9-84b3-2588b84b2aef","uuid":"589e33f1-c7b1-4007-94e5-c4ff642d9cf6","timestamp":"2026-09-25T08:52:21.060Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-5ccb419a44c1/apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/device-directory.service.ts","content":"import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { DataSource, EntityManager } from 'typeorm';\n\n/** Public identifier columns the directory can resolve a tenant by. */\nexport type DirectoryLookupColumn = 'device_code' | 'mqtt_client_id' | 'id';\n\n/** A row to (re)publish into the directory. */\nexport interface DeviceDirectoryEntry {\n  deviceId: string;\n  deviceCode: string;\n  mqttClientId?: string | null;\n  tenantId: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Device Directory Service (SENSOR-MEDIUM-004)\n *\n * Owns the cross-tenant `sensor.edge_device_directory` index that maps a\n * device's public identifiers to its owning tenant in O(1). Public\n * provisioning + MQTT-auth endpoints use it to resolve the tenant with a single\n * indexed query instead of a UNION-ALL scan across every tenant schema.\n *\n * The directory is a routing hint, not the source of truth: writers keep it in\n * sync inside the same transaction that mutates `edge_devices`, and readers\n * treat a miss as \"fall back to the scan and backfill me\".\n */\n@Injectable()\nexport class DeviceDirectoryService {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(DeviceDirectoryService.name);\n\n  // The directory column that a given edge_devices lookup column maps to.\n  private static readonly COLUMN_MAP: Record<DirectoryLookupColumn, string> = {\n    device_code: 'device_code',\n    mqtt_client_id: 'mqtt_client_id',\n    id: 'device_id',\n  };\n\n  constructor(private readonly dataSource: DataSource) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Resolve the owning tenantId for a device by one of its public identifiers,\n   * in O(1) via the directory index. Returns null on a miss (caller falls back\n   * to the cross-schema scan).\n   */\n  async lookupTenantId(column: DirectoryLookupColumn, value: string): Promise<string | null> {\n    const dirColumn = DeviceDirectoryService.COLUMN_MAP[column];\n    const rows: { tenant_id: string }[] = await this.dataSource.query(\n      `SELECT tenant_id FROM sensor.edge_device_directory WHERE \"${dirColumn}\" = $1 LIMIT 1`,\n      [value],\n    );\n    return rows[0]?.tenant_id ?? null;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Insert or refresh the directory row for a device. Keyed on device_id so a\n   * re-registration or identifier change updates in place. Runs inside the\n   * caller's transaction when a manager is supplied.\n   */\n  async upsert(entry: DeviceDirectoryEntry, manager?: EntityManager): Promise<void> {\n    const runner = manager ?? this.dataSource.manager;\n    await runner.query(\n      `INSERT INTO sensor.edge_device_directory\n         (device_id, device_code, mqtt_client_id, tenant_id, updated_at)\n       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, now())\n       ON CONFLICT (device_id) DO UPDATE SET\n         device_code = EXCLUDED.device_code,\n         mqtt_client_id = EXCLUDED.mqtt_client_id,\n         tenant_id = EXCLUDED.tenant_id,\n         updated_at = now()`,\n      [entry.deviceId, entry.deviceCode, entry.mqttClientId ?? null, entry.tenantId],\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Best-effort backfill from a device row a scan just resolved. Never throws —\n   * the caller already has its answer; a directory hiccup must not fail the\n   * request. Self-heals the directory for the next lookup.\n   */\n  async backfill(entry: DeviceDirectoryEntry): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await this.upsert(entry);\n    } catch (error) {\n      this.logger.warn(\n        `Directory backfill failed for device ${entry.deviceId}: ${(error as Error).message}`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** Remove a device from the directory (decommission / hard delete). */\n  async remove(deviceId: string, manager?: EntityManager): Promise<void> {","numLines":90,"startLine":1,"totalLines":95}}}

hand-typed\n457\t// annotations of that shape have already shipped one silent security-signal\n458\t// outage (ACCOUNT_LOCKED never fired).\n459\texport * from './update-returning.util';\n460\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"be4c4031-0af4-45d9-84b3-2588b84b2aef","uuid":"8637fedf-515a-4be8-91fe-3b22dc0a0ff5","timestamp":"2026-09-25T08:52:35.805Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-5ccb419a44c1/libs/backend-common/src/database/index.ts","content":"// Schema Manager\nexport * from './schema-manager.service';\nexport * from './recovery-point';\n\n// SQL identifier validator — single canonical helper for identifier\n// interpolation (DDL paths use it instead of inlining a private regex).\nexport { validateSqlIdentifier } from './sql-identifier.util';\n// Audit append-only contract SQL — one definition for all four audit tables.\nexport * from './audit-immutability.sql';\nexport type { SqlIdentifierKind } from './sql-identifier.util';\n\n// TypeORM driver query result normalizer — single canonical adapter for\n// raw `DataSource.query()` return shapes across Postgres and CLI/test drivers.\nexport {\n  queryResultNormalized,\n  queryRowCountNormalized,\n  queryRowsNormalized,\n  queryRowsWithStringColumn,\n  querySingleStringColumn,\n} from './query-result-normalizer';\nexport type { NormalizedQueryResult, StringColumnRow } from './query-result-normalizer';\n\n// Bounded, ctid-addressed age-based disposal (ADMIN-HIGH-013).\nexport * from './batched-delete';\n\n// Source Schema Bootstrap\nexport * from './source-schema-bootstrap.service';\n\n// Tenant-Aware Repository (legacy request-scoped, HTTP-only)\nexport * from './tenant-aware.repository';\n\n// Raw query result normalization. TypeORM + pg can return rows directly,\n// [rows, rowCount] tuples, or QueryResult-like objects depending on caller.\n// Lifecycle/provisioning code must not hand-roll this shape.\nexport * from './query-result-normalizer';\n\n// Tenant-Scoped Repository (AsyncLocalStorage-based, works in HTTP + MQTT + cron + NATS)\nexport * from './tenant-scoped-repository';\nexport * from './tenant-scoped-repository.module';\nexport * from './tenant-transaction';\nexport * from './tenant-context-error';\n\n// Transformers\nexport * from './decimal-transformer';\n\n// Schema LRU Cache (for tenant-schema middleware)\nexport * from './schema-lru-cache';\n\n// Shared tenant schema-existence cache (injectable singleton) + its\n// provisioning-driven invalidation subscriber + SSoT module. Imported once\n// per tenant-scoped service so TenantSchemaMiddleware and the TenantProvisioned\n// subscriber share one cache instance (no stale-negative-cache block for\n// freshly provisioned tenants).\nexport * from './tenant-schema-cache';\n\n// Tenant Schema Utilities (pure functions, no DI required)\nexport * from './tenant-schema.utils';\n\n// Fair, bounded per-tenant-schema cron/scheduler fan-out (cron-fairness).\n// Bounded concurrency + per-tenant Node+DB timeout + error isolation + rotation.\nexport * from './for-each-tenant-schema';\n\n// Migration Logger (structured logging for TypeORM migrations outside DI)\nexport { MigrationLogger } from './migration-logger';\n\n// Migration ledger SSoT. Keep every runner/gate/tenant seeding path on the\n// same TypeORM ledger table name.\nexport { MIGRATION_LEDGER_TABLE, tenantMigrationLedgerTable } from './migration-ledger';\n\n// Tenant migration ledger privilege SSoT. aqua-db-migrate creates/backfills\n// tenant ledgers; runtime services only need read access for SchemaVersionGate.\nexport {\n  buildTenantMigrationLedgerReadGrant,\n  grantTenantMigrationLedgerReadAccess,\n  serviceRoleForTenantAwareSchema,\n} from './tenant-migration-ledger-privileges';\nexport type {\n  TenantMigrationLedgerQueryExecutor,\n  TenantMigrationLedgerReadGrant,\n  TenantMigrationLedgerReadGrantOptions,\n} from './tenant-migration-ledger-privileges';\n// Tenant-schema table privilege SSoT (2026-07-06 grant incident): per-tenant\n// table clones must carry <source>_schema_owner ownership + <source>_service\n// DML regardless of which connection created them. assert = idempotent align;\n// verify = deploy/job-blocking drift detection.\nexport {\n  assertTenantSchemaPrivileges,\n  verifyTenantSchemaPrivileges,\n  ownerRoleForTenantAwareSchema,\n  serviceReadOnlyTenantTablesForSourceSchema,\n  tenantTablesForSourceSchema,\n} from './tenant-schema-privileges';\nexport type {\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeExecutor,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeOptions,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeReport,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeVerification,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeViolation,\n} from './tenant-schema-privileges';\nexport { grantTenantMessagingPartitionAuthority } from './messaging-partition-privileges';\nexport type {\n  MessagingPartitionAuthorityGrant,\n  MessagingPartitionAuthorityOptions,\n  MessagingPartitionAuthorityQueryExecutor,\n} from './messaging-partition-privileges';\n\n// Per-tenant sensor TimescaleDB rollup SSoT. db-migrate owns production DDL;\n// sensor runtime consumes the same names/SQL only for verification and local\n// non-authoritative development bootstrap.\nexport * from './sensor-continuous-aggregate-definition';\n\n// Migration helpers — column/table existence guards. Shared across all\n// services for migrations that reference state created by squashed\n// earlier migrations. See migration-helpers.ts docblock for rationale.\nexport { columnExists, tableExists } from './migration-helpers';\n\n// Transactional outbox DDL — one SQL shape for every service-local\n// outbox table that backs @platform/outbox. Migrations pass only schema,\n// table, and object names; column/index contract stays centralized.\n\n// SQL fragments — compile-time SQL injection prevention. Branded SqlIdent\n// + SqlFragment types make raw-string interpolation a TypeScript compile\n// error. Prereq for Phase 3 primitives rewrite (plan v3 R2 CRITICAL).\n// See sql-fragments.ts docblock.\nexport { sql, sqlGuards, executeSqlFragment } from './sql-fragments';\nexport type { SqlIdent, SqlValue, SqlFragment } from './sql-fragments';\n\n// Migration helpers (pinSearchPath, dropPartialTables,\n// parseAlterColumnTypeTargets, dropDependentPartialIndexes) — shared by\n// migration authors so search_path boilerplate, partial-state cleanup,\n// and ALTER-COLUMN-TYPE dependency resolution live in one place.\n// See base-migration.ts for rationale.\nexport {\n  pinSearchPath,\n  dropPartialTables,\n  parseAlterColumnTypeTargets,\n  dropDependentPartialIndexes,\n  withDdlSafety,\n} from './base-migration';\nexport type {\n  AlterColumnTypeTarget,\n  BlockingDependency,\n  BlockingDependencyKind,\n  /** @deprecated alias for {@link BlockingDependency} */\n  BlockingPartialIndex,\n  DdlSafetyOptions,\n} from './base-migration';\n\n// TENANT_AWARE_SCHEMAS — SSoT for schema-per-tenant services. Imported\n// by migration-runner.service.ts (boot-time fan-out), aqua-db-migrate\n// orchestrator (deploy-time fan-out), and schema-propagation.spec.ts\n// (CI invariant). See tenant-aware-schemas.ts for rationale.\nexport { TENANT_AWARE_SCHEMAS, TENANT_SCHEMA_NAME_RE } from './tenant-aware-schemas';\nexport {\n  SourceOnlyMigration,\n  getSourceOnlyMigrationMetadata,\n  isSourceOnlyMigration,\n} from './tenant-fanout.decorator';\nexport type {\n  SourceOnlyMigrationMetadata,\n  SourceOnlyMigrationOptions,\n} from './tenant-fanout.decorator';\n\n// Tenant Schema Sync (auto-provisioning)\nexport * from './tenant-schema-sync.service';\n\n// Source Schema Write Guard reconciler (DB-level tenant-isolation trigger SSoT)\nexport * from './source-schema-write-guard-reconciler';\n\n// Watchdog system (source contamination scanner, cross-tenant probe, drift detector)\nexport * from './watchdog';\n\n// Tenant Connection Bootstrap (centralized factory)\nexport { createTenantConnectionBootstrap } from './tenant-connection-bootstrap.service';\n\n// TypeORM bootstrap factory — single source of truth for every service's\n// TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync payload. Replaces 13 hand-rolled, drift-prone\n// app.module.ts blocks. See INFRA-DB-POOL-001 + the factory's docblock.\nexport {\n  createServiceTypeOrmConfig,\n  DEFAULT_POOL_SIZE,\n  DEFAULT_POOL_MIN,\n  DEFAULT_POOL_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS,\n  DEFAULT_POOL_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS,\n} from './typeorm-config.factory';\nexport type { ServiceTypeOrmOptions } from './typeorm-config.factory';\n\n// SSL config helper — extracted previously but never wired in. Now consumed\n// by the TypeORM factory above. Re-exported so direct callers (e.g. raw\n// DataSource construction in CLI tools) can use the same logic.\nexport { buildDatabaseSslConfig } from './ssl-config';\nexport type { SslConfigResult } from './ssl-config';\n\n// Row-Level Security (RLS) for PostgreSQL tenant isolation\nexport * from './rls';\n\n// Migration runner factory — produces an OnApplicationBootstrap provider\n// that runs pending TypeORM migrations with a runner-enforced search_path\n// invariant. Shared across every service that wires TypeORM migrations\n// (farm, hr, messaging, sensor, billing, config, notification, alert, ai,\n// event-store); each calls the factory with its own source schema name.\nexport * from './migration-runner';\n\n// MigrationRunnerModule — Phase 6 platform wiring wrapper. Services\n// import `MigrationRunnerModule.forRoot({schema: 'hr'})` instead of\n// pasting the factory providers block. Auto-threads the MigrationEventSink\n// + ConfigService through Nest DI.\nexport { MigrationRunnerModule } from './migration-runner/migration-runner.module';\nexport type { MigrationRunnerModuleOptions } from './migration-runner/migration-runner.module';\n\n// SchemaVersionGate — Faz 1.5 of day-one baseline reset + ADR-021.\n// Strict superset of createMigrationRunnerService: in production-like\n// envs (DB_MIGRATE_AUTHORITATIVE=true) runs as a read-only probe that\n// refuses boot if aqua-db-migrate has not finalised the ledger. In dev\n// (default) delegates to the runner verbatim. Use this factory in every\n// service's app.module.ts instead of createMigrationRunnerService —\n// production safety + dev ergonomics in a single provider class.\nexport { createSchemaVersionGate } from './schema-version-gate.service';\nexport type { SchemaVersionGateOptions } from './schema-version-gate.service';\n\n// Retention — single-enforcer-many-policies pattern (plan v3 R17).\n// Tables with SOC2 / KVKK retention windows register a policy at\n// module-init; RetentionEnforcementService iterates all on a daily\n// cron. See retention/retention-policy.ts docblock.\nexport * from './retention';\n\n// assertExpandContractDependency — R6 runtime gate. Called by\n// MigrationRunnerService before executing each migration; contract-phase\n// classes MUST have their dependsOn expand migration recorded in\n// observability.migration_backfill_progress for the environment.\n// See assert-expand-contract-dependency.ts docblock.\nexport { assertExpandContractDependency } from './assert-expand-contract-dependency';\nexport type {\n  AssertDependencyOptions,\n  AssertDependencyResult,\n} from './assert-expand-contract-dependency';\n\n// Schema drift validator — OnApplicationBootstrap provider factory that\n// compares entity metadata against information_schema on every boot and\n// fails fast on divergence (uuid→text drift, wrong schema, nullability\n// mismatch). Would have caught the 2026-04-14 audit_logs.tenantId drift\n// weeks before it broke RLS in production. Wire via SchemaDriftModule.forRoot()\n// in each service's AppModule (consistent with RlsModule.forPoolService() pattern).\n// The bare factory is exported for advanced wiring (e.g. tests with mocked\n// DataSource) but services should prefer the module API.\nexport { createSchemaDriftValidator } from './schema-drift-validator.service';\nexport { SchemaDriftModule } from './schema-drift/schema-drift.module';\nexport type { SchemaDriftModuleOptions } from './schema-drift/schema-drift.module';\n\n// Drift-class registry — single source of truth for validator ↔ primitive\n// parity. See docs/plans/2026-04-21-db-migrate-enterprise-refactor.md §R11\n// + libs/backend-common/src/database/schema-drift/drift-classes.ts docblock.\nexport { DRIFT_CLASSES, DRIFT_CLASS_LIST, isDriftClassId } from './schema-drift/drift-classes';\nexport type { DriftClassId, DriftClassSpec, DriftSeverity } from './schema-drift/drift-classes';\nexport {\n  expectedEntityDbType,\n  isUuidTypeDrift,\n  normalizeInformationSchemaType,\n} from './schema-drift/type-normalization';\nexport type {\n  EntityColumnTypeShape,\n  InformationSchemaColumnShape,\n} from './schema-drift/type-normalization';\n\n// pg_catalog introspector — normalized ORM-agnostic snapshot of a PG\n// schema. Consumed by SchemaDriftValidator + Phase 4 PR gate. Replaces\n// TypeORM's createSchemaBuilder().log() which is known to miss\n// partial-index predicates, EXCLUDE operator classes, GIN opclass.\nexport { introspectSchema } from './schema-drift/pg-catalog-introspector';\nexport type {\n  IntrospectedCheckConstraint,\n  IntrospectedColumn,\n  IntrospectedEnum,\n  IntrospectedTable,\n  IntrospectedPartialIndex,\n  IntrospectedExcludeConstraint,\n  IntrospectedForeignKeyAction,\n  IntrospectedGeneratedColumn,\n  IntrospectedHypertable,\n  IntrospectedRlsPolicy,\n  SchemaSnapshot,\n} from './schema-drift/pg-catalog-introspector';\n\n// Phase 4 PR-gate foundation — pairwise snapshot diff + severity\n// partitioning. Pure, side-effect-free; consumed by the CI diff\n// script that compares pre-merge vs post-migrate shadow snapshots.\nexport { diffSnapshots, partitionBySeverity } from './schema-drift/diff-snapshots';\nexport type {\n  SnapshotChange,\n  SnapshotChangeKind,\n  SnapshotChangeSeverity,\n} from './schema-drift/diff-snapshots';\n\n// Phase 7 R14 — snapshot PII scrubber. Produces a redacted copy of a\n// SchemaSnapshot suitable for cross-region upload or public channels.\nexport { scrubSnapshot, DEFAULT_PII_COLUMN_NAMES } from './schema-drift/snapshot-scrubber';\nexport type { ScrubbedSnapshot, ScrubOptions } from './schema-drift/snapshot-scrubber';\n\n// Phase 3 primitives — declarative schema healers for drift classes\n// A-G. Each primitive composes over withDdlSafety and sql.* branded\n// fragments; raw-string SQL is a compile error at the call site.\n// addMissingColumns heals Class D (entity declares column, DB lacks).\n// alignColumnNullability heals Class C (entity NOT NULL, DB nullable).\nexport { addMissingColumns } from './schema-primitives/add-missing-columns';\nexport type {\n  AddMissingColumnSpec,\n  AddMissingColumnsOptions,\n  AddMissingColumnsResult,\n} from './schema-primitives/add-missing-columns';\nexport { alignColumnNullability } from './schema-primitives/align-column-nullability';\nexport type {\n  AlignColumnNullabilitySpec,\n  AlignColumnNullabilityOptions,\n  AlignColumnNullabilityResult,\n} from './schema-primitives/align-column-nullability';\nexport { alignEnumLabels } from './schema-primitives/align-enum-labels';\nexport type {\n  AlignEnumLabelsTarget,\n  AlignEnumLabelsOptions,\n  AlignEnumLabelsResult,\n} from './schema-primitives/align-enum-labels';\nexport { dropOrphanedColumns } from './schema-primitives/drop-orphaned-columns';\nexport type {\n  DropOrphanedColumnsOptions,\n  DropOrphanedColumnsResult,\n} from './schema-primitives/drop-orphaned-columns';\nexport { alignCheckConstraints } from './schema-primitives/align-check-constraints';\nexport type {\n  CheckConstraintSpec,\n  AlignCheckConstraintsOptions,\n  AlignCheckConstraintsResult,\n} from './schema-primitives/align-check-constraints';\nexport { alignColumnType } from './schema-primitives/align-column-type';\nexport type {\n  AlignColumnTypeSpec,\n  AlignColumnTypeOptions,\n  AlignColumnTypeResult,\n} from './schema-primitives/align-column-type';\n// Phase 3.5 — chunked Class H backfill primitive (large-table safe).\nexport { backfillColumn } from './schema-primitives/backfill-column';\nexport type {\n  BackfillColumnOptions,\n  BackfillColumnResult,\n  BackfillProgress,\n} from './schema-primitives/backfill-column';\n\n// Emergency override runtime read — aqua-ctl + validator integration\n// point. Lookups observability.emergency_overrides for ACTIVE rows\n// matching (service, kind, environment). Fail-safe: lookup errors\n// never grant bypass. See emergency-override-check.ts docblock.\nexport { lookupEmergencyOverride } from './emergency-override-check';\nexport type {\n  EmergencyOverrideKind,\n  EmergencyOverrideRow,\n  EmergencyOverrideLookupResult,\n  EmergencyOverrideLookupOptions,\n} from './emergency-override-check';\n\n// MigrationEventSink — decoupled hook for lifecycle-event emission from\n// the per-service MigrationRunnerService. Phase 6 integration layer.\n// See migration-event-sink.ts for the three provided implementations\n// (NoopMigrationEventSink, InMemoryMigrationEventSink for tests,\n// LoggerMigrationEventSink for dev/staging).\nexport {\n  NoopMigrationEventSink,\n  InMemoryMigrationEventSink,\n  LoggerMigrationEventSink,\n} from './migration-event-sink';\nexport type {\n  MigrationEventSink,\n  MigrationSinkEvent,\n  MigrationSinkEventType,\n} from './migration-event-sink';\n// NATS bridge publisher — Phase 6 Step 5. Translates lifecycle events\n// into SchemaMigrationEvent wire shape + publishes under\n// SCHEMA_MIGRATION_SUBJECT_PREFIX. Observability-service consumer\n// subscribes in Step 6.\nexport { NatsMigrationEventSink } from './nats-migration-event-sink';\nexport type {\n  MigrationEventPublisher,\n  NatsMigrationEventSinkOptions,\n} from './nats-migration-event-sink';\n\n// @TenantFanOut + @AllowTenantDelta — Phase 6 R21/R24 migration-class\n// metadata. Orchestrator reads fan-out policy; Class I drift check\n// reads tenant delta allowlist. See tenant-fanout.decorator.ts.\nexport {\n  TenantFanOut,\n  AllowTenantDelta,\n  TENANT_FANOUT_META_KEY,\n  ALLOW_TENANT_DELTA_META_KEY,\n  getTenantFanOutMetadata,\n  getAllowedTenantDeltaPrefixes,\n  isTenantDeltaAllowed,\n} from './tenant-fanout.decorator';\nexport type {\n  TenantLockClass,\n  TenantFanOutOptions,\n  TenantFanOutMetadata,\n  AllowTenantDeltaOptions,\n  AllowTenantDeltaMetadata,\n} from './tenant-fanout.decorator';\n\n// @ExpandContract — declarative marker for blue-green migration phases.\n// Phase 4 PR-gate reads this metadata to authorize breaking diffs on\n// contract-phase migrations. See expand-contract.decorator.ts.\nexport {\n  ExpandContract,\n  EXPAND_CONTRACT_META_KEY,\n  getExpandContractMetadata,\n  authorizesBreaking,\n  classifyMigrationsForBreaking,\n} from './expand-contract.decorator';\nexport type {\n  ExpandContractPhase,\n  ExpandContractOptions,\n  ExpandContractMetadata,\n  MigrationClassification,\n  BatchClassificationResult,\n} from './expand-contract.decorator';\n\n// @EncryptedAtRest — declarative marker for cryptographically-encrypted\n// columns. Drift validator Class J enforces bytea storage; Phase 3\n// primitives refuse DDL against decorated columns. See ADR-023.\nexport {\n  EncryptedAtRest,\n  ENCRYPTED_AT_REST_META_KEY,\n  getEncryptedAtRestMetadata,\n  getEncryptedAtRestForProperty,\n} from './encrypted-at-rest.decorator';\nexport type {\n  EncryptionAlgorithm,\n  EncryptedAtRestOptions,\n  EncryptedAtRestMetadata,\n} from './encrypted-at-rest.decorator';\n\n// Audit-column TIMESTAMP → TIMESTAMPTZ conversion (NEW-H1).\n// `convertAuditColumnsToTimestamptz` and `revertAuditColumnsToTimestamp`\n// are imported by per-service migrations in `auth`, `admin-api`, `farm`,\n// `sensor`, and `messaging`. Under DB_MIGRATE_AUTHORITATIVE deployments,\n// audit hardening belongs in aqua-db-migrate postMigrationHardening; the\n// runtime `AuditColumnsModule` is a local/dev convenience only.\nexport * from './convert-audit-columns-to-timestamptz.helper';\nexport * from './audit-columns-bootstrap.service';\nexport * from './audit-columns.module';\nexport * from './db-migrate-authority.util';\n\n// DATA-LOW-001 cure: typed pg.Pool extractor that hides the\n// single `as any` driver-shape bridge in one canonical adapter.\n// Connection-bootstrap services import this instead of casting\n// dataSource.driver inline.\nexport * from './pg-pool-from-data-source.util';\n\n// ORPHAN-HIGH-318 cure: runtime-asserted reader for raw\n// `UPDATE … RETURNING` results through dataSource.query() — the postgres\n// driver returns [rows, affectedCount], not the rows array; hand-typed\n// annotations of that shape have already shipped one silent security-signal\n// outage (ACCOUNT_LOCKED never fired).\nexport * from './update-returning.util';\n","numLines":460,"startLine":1,"totalLines":460}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KZRdR2x3PVdR7CDK865kmd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { DataSource } from 'typeorm';\n2\t\n3\t/**\n4\t * UUID format validator (any version). File-local; not exported to avoid\n5\t * a name collision with `security/validators/regex-patterns.ts::UUID_V4_REGEX`\n6\t * (which is strictly v4) now that both subtrees are `export *`-barreled\n7\t * through the root. Previously the old root barrel used explicit named\n8\t * re-exports that masked the duplication. Consumers that need UUID matching\n9\t * outside this file should import from either:\n10\t *   - `@aquaculture/backend-common/constants` (UUID_REGEX, v1-v5, preferred)\n11\t *   - `@aquaculture/backend-common/security` (UUID_V4_REGEX, v4-only)\n12\t *\n13\t * This regex accepts any-version UUIDs and exists solely to back the\n14\t * `isValidUUID()` helper below — callers who want stricter v4 checking\n15\t * should use `UUID_V4_REGEX` from the security sub-barrel.\n16\t */\n17\tconst UUID_ANY_VERSION_REGEX = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;\n18\t\n19\t/**\n20\t * Schema name validation regex.\n21\t * Ensures schema names contain only safe characters for SQL interpolation.\n22\t */\n23\texport const SCHEMA_NAME_REGEX = /^[a-z0-9_]+$/;\n24\t\n25\texport interface TenantSchemaIdentity {\n26\t  schemaName: string;\n27\t  tenantId: string;\n28\t}\n29\t\n30\t/**\n31\t * Validate whether a string is a valid UUID v4 format.\n32\t */\n33\texport function isValidUUID(id: string): boolean {\n34\t  return UUID_ANY_VERSION_REGEX.test(id);\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\t/**\n38\t * Validates a schema name contains only safe characters.\n39\t */\n40\texport function isValidSchemaName(name: string): boolean {\n41\t  return SCHEMA_NAME_REGEX.test(name);\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\t/**\n45\t * Assert that a schema name is safe for SQL interpolation — throws on failure.\n46\t *\n47\t * Use this in migrations that interpolate schema names from information_schema\n48\t * into SQL templates. Although information_schema.schemata is a trusted source,\n49\t * defense-in-depth requires validation before any SQL identifier interpolation.\n50\t *\n51\t * @throws Error if the schema name contains unsafe characters or is too long.\n52\t */\n53\texport function assertSafeSchemaName(name: string): void {\n54\t  if (!SCHEMA_NAME_REGEX.test(name) || name.length > 63) {\n55\t    throw new Error(\n56\t      `SECURITY: Unsafe schema name '${name}' rejected before SQL interpolation. ` +\n57\t        'Schema names must match ^[a-z0-9_]+$ and be ≤63 chars.',\n58\t    );\n59\t  }\n60\t}\n61\t\n62\t/**\n63\t * Derive the PostgreSQL schema name from a tenant UUID.\n64\t *\n65\t * Format: tenant_{first16_hex_chars_of_uuid_without_dashes}\n66\t * Example: \"4b529829-ea79-48da-982c-cd6fbec8ffb7\" -> \"tenant_4b529829ea7948da\"\n67\t *\n68\t * This is a pure function (no DB access, no DI required) so it can be used\n69\t * in middleware, NATS handlers, cron jobs, and MQTT listeners without\n70\t * injecting SchemaManagerService.\n71\t *\n72\t * MUST stay in sync with SchemaManagerService.getTenantSchemaName().\n73\t * SchemaManagerService adds UUID validation and throws BadRequestException;\n74\t * this function does NOT validate -- callers must validate before calling.\n75\t */\n76\texport function getTenantSchemaName(tenantId: string): string {\n77\t  const cleanId = tenantId.replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 16).toLowerCase();\n78\t  return `tenant_${cleanId}`;\n79\t}\n80\t\n81\t/**\n82\t * Query all tenant schema names from information_schema.\n83\t *\n84\t * Used by cron jobs and schedulers that need to iterate over all tenant\n85\t * schemas. Requires a TypeORM DataSource (no NestJS DI dependency).\n86\t *\n87\t * @param dataSource - TypeORM DataSource instance\n88\t * @returns Sorted array of schema names matching 'tenant_%'\n89\t */\n90\texport async function listTenantSchemas(dataSource: DataSource): Promise<string[]> {\n91\t  const rows: { schema_name: string }[] = await dataSource.query(\n92\t    `SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata\n93\t     WHERE schema_name ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n94\t     ORDER BY schema_name`,\n95\t  );\n96\t  return rows.map((r) => r.schema_name);\n97\t}\n98\t\n99\t/**\n100\t * Resolve provisioned tenant schemas to their full tenant UUIDs through the\n101\t * db-migrate-owned commit ledger.\n102\t *\n103\t * A schema name contains only the first 16 UUID hex characters, so it cannot\n104\t * be reversed into the RLS identity required by `runInTenantTransaction`.\n105\t * Cross-tenant workers must use this mapping instead of guessing an ID or\n106\t * enabling an unrestricted RLS bypass. The SECURITY DEFINER function exposes\n107\t * only active `{schema_name, tenant_id}` pairs and keeps direct access to\n108\t * `admin.tenant_schemas` outside runtime service roles.\n109\t */\n110\texport async function listActiveTenantSchemaIdentities(\n111\t  dataSource: DataSource,\n112\t): Promise<TenantSchemaIdentity[]> {\n113\t  return listVerifiedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n114\t    dataSource,\n115\t    'platform.list_active_tenant_schema_mappings()',\n116\t    'active',\n117\t  );\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\t/**\n121\t * Resolve every committed physical schema that still retains tenant data.\n122\t *\n123\t * Lifecycle work (secret scrubbing, retention and erasure) must continue for\n124\t * suspended, migrating and pending-deletion tenants even though provider\n125\t * ingestion is intentionally active-only. The platform wrapper is\n126\t * least-privileged and excludes creating/deleted ledger rows.\n127\t */\n128\texport async function listRetainedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n129\t  dataSource: DataSource,\n130\t): Promise<TenantSchemaIdentity[]> {\n131\t  return listVerifiedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n132\t    dataSource,\n133\t    'platform.list_retained_tenant_schema_mappings()',\n134\t    'retained',\n135\t  );\n136\t}\n137\t\n138\ttype TenantSchemaMappingFunction =\n139\t  | 'platform.list_active_tenant_schema_mappings()'\n140\t  | 'platform.list_retained_tenant_schema_mappings()';\n141\t\n142\tasync function listVerifiedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n143\t  dataSource: DataSource,\n144\t  mappingFunction: TenantSchemaMappingFunction,\n145\t  mappingKind: 'active' | 'retained',\n146\t): Promise<TenantSchemaIdentity[]> {\n147\t  const rows: Array<{\n148\t    schema_name: string;\n149\t    tenant_id: string;\n150\t    schema_exists: boolean;\n151\t    committed_proof: boolean;\n152\t  }> = await dataSource.query(\n153\t    `SELECT schema_name, tenant_id::text AS tenant_id\n154\t            , schema_exists, committed_proof\n155\t       FROM ${mappingFunction}\n156\t      ORDER BY schema_name`,\n157\t  );\n158\t\n159\t  const schemas = new Set<string>();\n160\t  const tenants = new Set<string>();\n161\t  return rows.map((row) => {\n162\t    assertSafeSchemaName(row.schema_name);\n163\t    if (row.schema_exists !== true) {\n164\t      throw new Error(\n165\t        `${mappingKind} tenant schema ledger entry \"${row.schema_name}\" has no physical schema`,\n166\t      );\n167\t    }\n168\t    if (row.committed_proof !== true) {\n169\t      throw new Error(\n170\t        `${mappingKind} tenant schema ledger entry \"${row.schema_name}\" has no matching committed operation`,\n171\t      );\n172\t    }\n173\t    if (!isValidUUID(row.tenant_id)) {\n174\t      throw new Error(\n175\t        `Tenant schema ledger returned an invalid tenant UUID for \"${row.schema_name}\"`,\n176\t      );\n177\t    }\n178\t    if (getTenantSchemaName(row.tenant_id) !== row.schema_name) {\n179\t      throw new Error(`Tenant schema ledger mapping mismatch for \"${row.schema_name}\"`);\n180\t    }\n181\t    const normalizedTenantId = row.tenant_id.toLowerCase();\n182\t    if (schemas.has(row.schema_name) || tenants.has(normalizedTenantId)) {\n183\t      throw new Error(`Tenant schema ledger returned a duplicate ${mappingKind} mapping`);\n184\t    }\n185\t    schemas.add(row.schema_name);\n186\t    tenants.add(normalizedTenantId);\n187\t    return {\n188\t      schemaName: row.schema_name,\n189\t      tenantId: normalizedTenantId,\n190\t    };\n191\t  });\n192\t}\n193\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"be4c4031-0af4-45d9-84b3-2588b84b2aef","uuid":"c297b6f2-7590-4c34-b4ec-1090ae353424","timestamp":"2026-09-25T08:52:39.463Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-5ccb419a44c1/libs/backend-common/src/database/tenant-schema.utils.ts","content":"import { DataSource } from 'typeorm';\n\n/**\n * UUID format validator (any version). File-local; not exported to avoid\n * a name collision with `security/validators/regex-patterns.ts::UUID_V4_REGEX`\n * (which is strictly v4) now that both subtrees are `export *`-barreled\n * through the root. Previously the old root barrel used explicit named\n * re-exports that masked the duplication. Consumers that need UUID matching\n * outside this file should import from either:\n *   - `@aquaculture/backend-common/constants` (UUID_REGEX, v1-v5, preferred)\n *   - `@aquaculture/backend-common/security` (UUID_V4_REGEX, v4-only)\n *\n * This regex accepts any-version UUIDs and exists solely to back the\n * `isValidUUID()` helper below — callers who want stricter v4 checking\n * should use `UUID_V4_REGEX` from the security sub-barrel.\n */\nconst UUID_ANY_VERSION_REGEX = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;\n\n/**\n * Schema name validation regex.\n * Ensures schema names contain only safe characters for SQL interpolation.\n */\nexport const SCHEMA_NAME_REGEX = /^[a-z0-9_]+$/;\n\nexport interface TenantSchemaIdentity {\n  schemaName: string;\n  tenantId: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Validate whether a string is a valid UUID v4 format.\n */\nexport function isValidUUID(id: string): boolean {\n  return UUID_ANY_VERSION_REGEX.test(id);\n}\n\n/**\n * Validates a schema name contains only safe characters.\n */\nexport function isValidSchemaName(name: string): boolean {\n  return SCHEMA_NAME_REGEX.test(name);\n}\n\n/**\n * Assert that a schema name is safe for SQL interpolation — throws on failure.\n *\n * Use this in migrations that interpolate schema names from information_schema\n * into SQL templates. Although information_schema.schemata is a trusted source,\n * defense-in-depth requires validation before any SQL identifier interpolation.\n *\n * @throws Error if the schema name contains unsafe characters or is too long.\n */\nexport function assertSafeSchemaName(name: string): void {\n  if (!SCHEMA_NAME_REGEX.test(name) || name.length > 63) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `SECURITY: Unsafe schema name '${name}' rejected before SQL interpolation. ` +\n        'Schema names must match ^[a-z0-9_]+$ and be ≤63 chars.',\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Derive the PostgreSQL schema name from a tenant UUID.\n *\n * Format: tenant_{first16_hex_chars_of_uuid_without_dashes}\n * Example: \"4b529829-ea79-48da-982c-cd6fbec8ffb7\" -> \"tenant_4b529829ea7948da\"\n *\n * This is a pure function (no DB access, no DI required) so it can be used\n * in middleware, NATS handlers, cron jobs, and MQTT listeners without\n * injecting SchemaManagerService.\n *\n * MUST stay in sync with SchemaManagerService.getTenantSchemaName().\n * SchemaManagerService adds UUID validation and throws BadRequestException;\n * this function does NOT validate -- callers must validate before calling.\n */\nexport function getTenantSchemaName(tenantId: string): string {\n  const cleanId = tenantId.replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 16).toLowerCase();\n  return `tenant_${cleanId}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Query all tenant schema names from information_schema.\n *\n * Used by cron jobs and schedulers that need to iterate over all tenant\n * schemas. Requires a TypeORM DataSource (no NestJS DI dependency).\n *\n * @param dataSource - TypeORM DataSource instance\n * @returns Sorted array of schema names matching 'tenant_%'\n */\nexport async function listTenantSchemas(dataSource: DataSource): Promise<string[]> {\n  const rows: { schema_name: string }[] = await dataSource.query(\n    `SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata\n     WHERE schema_name ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n     ORDER BY schema_name`,\n  );\n  return rows.map((r) => r.schema_name);\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve provisioned tenant schemas to their full tenant UUIDs through the\n * db-migrate-owned commit ledger.\n *\n * A schema name contains only the first 16 UUID hex characters, so it cannot\n * be reversed into the RLS identity required by `runInTenantTransaction`.\n * Cross-tenant workers must use this mapping instead of guessing an ID or\n * enabling an unrestricted RLS bypass. The SECURITY DEFINER function exposes\n * only active `{schema_name, tenant_id}` pairs and keeps direct access to\n * `admin.tenant_schemas` outside runtime service roles.\n */\nexport async function listActiveTenantSchemaIdentities(\n  dataSource: DataSource,\n): Promise<TenantSchemaIdentity[]> {\n  return listVerifiedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n    dataSource,\n    'platform.list_active_tenant_schema_mappings()',\n    'active',\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve every committed physical schema that still retains tenant data.\n *\n * Lifecycle work (secret scrubbing, retention and erasure) must continue for\n * suspended, migrating and pending-deletion tenants even though provider\n * ingestion is intentionally active-only. The platform wrapper is\n * least-privileged and excludes creating/deleted ledger rows.\n */\nexport async function listRetainedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n  dataSource: DataSource,\n): Promise<TenantSchemaIdentity[]> {\n  return listVerifiedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n    dataSource,\n    'platform.list_retained_tenant_schema_mappings()',\n    'retained',\n  );\n}\n\ntype TenantSchemaMappingFunction =\n  | 'platform.list_active_tenant_schema_mappings()'\n  | 'platform.list_retained_tenant_schema_mappings()';\n\nasync function listVerifiedTenantSchemaIdentities(\n  dataSource: DataSource,\n  mappingFunction: TenantSchemaMappingFunction,\n  mappingKind: 'active' | 'retained',\n): Promise<TenantSchemaIdentity[]> {\n  const rows: Array<{\n    schema_name: string;\n    tenant_id: string;\n    schema_exists: boolean;\n    committed_proof: boolean;\n  }> = await dataSource.query(\n    `SELECT schema_name, tenant_id::text AS tenant_id\n            , schema_exists, committed_proof\n       FROM ${mappingFunction}\n      ORDER BY schema_name`,\n  );\n\n  const schemas = new Set<string>();\n  const tenants = new Set<string>();\n  return rows.map((row) => {\n    assertSafeSchemaName(row.schema_name);\n    if (row.schema_exists !== true) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `${mappingKind} tenant schema ledger entry \"${row.schema_name}\" has no physical schema`,\n      );\n    }\n    if (row.committed_proof !== true) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `${mappingKind} tenant schema ledger entry \"${row.schema_name}\" has no matching committed operation`,\n      );\n    }\n    if (!isValidUUID(row.tenant_id)) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Tenant schema ledger returned an invalid tenant UUID for \"${row.schema_name}\"`,\n      );\n    }\n    if (getTenantSchemaName(row.tenant_id) !== row.schema_name) {\n      throw new Error(`Tenant schema ledger mapping mismatch for \"${row.schema_name}\"`);\n    }\n    const normalizedTenantId = row.tenant_id.toLowerCase();\n    if (schemas.has(row.schema_name) || tenants.has(normalizedTenantId)) {\n      throw new Error(`Tenant schema ledger returned a duplicate ${mappingKind} mapping`);\n    }\n    schemas.add(row.schema_name);\n    tenants.add(normalizedTenantId);\n    return {\n      schemaName: row.schema_name,\n      tenantId: normalizedTenantId,\n    };\n  });\n}\n","numLines":193,"startLine":1,"totalLines":193}}}
